.
For simplicity, we often just write
\mathbf{N}_{s}
when the ambient space is clear from the context.
The family
\mathcal{B}=\{\mathbf{N}_{s}\mid s\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-12.473pt{<\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-29.16072pt\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-12.473pt{<\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-29.16072pt\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.3204pt{<\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-20.24023pt\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-7.2704pt{<\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-19.19023pt\mathchoice{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.72676pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.34798pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.53429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}\}
consists of clopen sets and is closed under intersections of size less than
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
.
Therefore,
\mathcal{B}
is a basis for the bounded topology on
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
; we call it the
canonical basis
.
For any subspace
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, we also denote by
\mathcal{B}(X)
the canonical basis of
X
consisting of the clopen cones
\mathbf{N}_{s}(X)=\mathbf{N}_{s}\cap X
such that
\mathbf{N}_{s}\cap X\neq\emptyset
.
The closure of
\mathcal{B}
(and
\mathcal{B}(X)
) under intersections of size less than
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
ensures that the bounded topology itself is closed under intersections of size less than
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
, that is, it is
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
-additive
.

The following well-known fact (see, e.g.,
[
22
, Theorem 2.7]
or
[
3
, Proposition 2.2.24]
) shows that the bounded topology is also zero-dimensional in a strong sense: every open cover can be refined by a clopen partition consisting of basic clopen cones.

Fact 2.1
.

Let
\gamma
be an ordinal, let
A
be a set, and let
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. For every family
\mathcal{V}
of open sets of
X
there is a family
\mathcal{P}\subseteq\mathcal{B}(X)
of disjoint cones which refines
\mathcal{V}
and satisfies that
\bigcup\mathcal{P}=\bigcup\mathcal{V}
.

Recall that, given a cardinal
\delta
, a space is called
\delta
-Lindelöf
if every open cover has a subcover of size strictly less than
\delta
.
From Fact
2.1
we can obtain the following immediate corollary.

Corollary 2.2
.

Let
\gamma
be an ordinal, let
A
be a set, and let
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. The following are equivalent:

(1)

X
is not
\lambda
-Lindelöf.

(2)

X
contains a clopen partition of size at least
\lambda
.

(3)

X
contains a closed discrete set of size (at least)
\lambda
.

Proof.

(1)
\Rightarrow
(2)
. Let
\mathcal{U}
be an open cover that does not contain any subcover of size smaller than
\lambda
. Let
\mathcal{P}
be a partition of
X
refining
\mathcal{U}
as given by Fact
2.1
. If
|\mathcal{P}|<\lambda
, then choosing
U_{P}\in\mathcal{U}
such that
P\subseteq U_{P}
for every
P\in\mathcal{P}
would lead to a contradiction. Therefore,
|\mathcal{P}|\geq\lambda
.

(2)
\Rightarrow
(3)
. Let
\mathcal{P}
be a partition of
X
of size (at least)
\lambda
, and choose
x_{p}\in P
for every
P\in\mathcal{P}
. Then
\{x_{P}\mid P\in\mathcal{P}\}
is closed and discrete, as wanted.

(3)
\Rightarrow
(1)
. It is enough to notice that every closed subspace of a
\lambda
-Lindelöf space is again
\lambda
-Lindelöf, while a discrete space of size
\lambda
is not
\lambda
-Lindelöf.
∎

Recall that the weight
\weight
of a topological space is the minimal size of a basis for its topology; the density
\density
is the minimal size of a dense subset; and the cellularity
\cellularity
is the minimal cardinal
\delta
such that every family of pairwise disjoint open sets has size at most
\delta
.

It is well known (see, e.g.,
[
9
, Exercise 1.7.12(a)]
) that for every topological space
X
we have
\cellularity(X)\leq\density(X)\leq\weight(X)
.
In any subspace
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
with the bounded topology, these three cardinals coincide.

Proposition 2.3
.

Let
\gamma
be an ordinal, let
A
be a set, and let
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. If
X
has weight
\lambda
, then it contains a family of pairwise disjoint basic clopen sets of size
\lambda
.

In particular,
\cellularity(X)=\density(X)=\weight(X)
.

Proof.

If
X
is discrete we are done, so suppose not. Then,
\gamma
is limit and
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
is infinite, and we must have
\kappa\leq\lambda
, since
X
is
\kappa
-additive and not discrete.

If
\lambda=\kappa
we are done: for any non-isolated point
x\in X
, we have that for cofinally many
\alpha<\gamma
the set
\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction\alpha}(X)\setminus\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction\alpha+1}(X)
must be non-empty. Let
\{\alpha_{i}\mid i<\kappa\}
be an enumeration of (some of) these
\alpha
s. Since
\mathcal{B}(X)
is a basis for
X
and
\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction\alpha+1}(X)\setminus\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction\alpha}(X)
is clopen, for every
i<\kappa
there is
s_{i}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.48848pt{<\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-28.80609pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.48848pt{<\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-28.80609pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.70824pt{<\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-20.07797pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-6.89157pt{<\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-19.2613pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
such that
\mathbf{N}_{s_{i}}(X)\neq\emptyset
and
\mathbf{N}_{s_{i}}(X)\subseteq\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction{\alpha_{i}}}(X)\setminus\mathbf{N}_{x\restriction{\alpha_{i}+1}}(X)
, which gives a family
\{\mathbf{N}_{s_{i}}(X)\mid i<\kappa\}
of non-empty disjoint basic clopen sets of size
\kappa=\lambda
, as wanted.

Therefore, assume
\kappa<\lambda
. Let
\langle\gamma_{\alpha}\mid\alpha<\kappa\rangle
be a cofinal sequence in
\gamma
.
Then, the family

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}(X)=\{\mathbf{N}_{s}(X)\mid s\in\bigcup_{\alpha<\kappa}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.33713pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-16.95816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-7.28712pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-15.90816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}\}\subseteq\mathcal{B}(X)

is still a basis for
X
.
Since
X
has weight
\lambda
, we can find
T\subseteq\bigcup_{\alpha<\kappa}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.33713pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-16.95816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-7.28712pt{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-15.90816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
such that
\mathbf{N}_{s}(X)=\mathbf{N}_{s}\cap X\neq\emptyset
for every
s\in T
and

\mathcal{B}^{\prime}=\{\mathbf{N}_{s}(X)\mid s\in T\}\subseteq\tilde{\mathcal{B}}(X)

is a basis for
X
of minimal size
|\mathcal{B}^{\prime}|=|T|=\lambda
.
Without loss of generality, we may assume that
s\restriction\gamma_{\alpha}\in T
for every
s\in T
and
\alpha<\kappa
.
For every
\alpha<\kappa
, let
\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-11.40085pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-23.76585pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.33713pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-16.95816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-7.28712pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-15.90816pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}\cap T
. If there is
\alpha<\kappa
such that
|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)|=\lambda
we are done, so suppose not.
Then,
|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)|<\lambda
for every
\alpha<\kappa
, and
\lambda=\sup_{\alpha<\kappa}|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)|
, thus
\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)=\kappa<\lambda
.

Proceeding recursively, we can build a cofinal set
I\subseteq\kappa
such that

\kappa<|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)|<|\mathrm{Lev}_{\beta}(T)|<\lambda

for every
\alpha,\beta\in I
satisfying
\alpha<\beta
. Let
I=\{\alpha_{i}\mid i<\kappa\}
be an increasing enumeration of it.
Since for every
s\in T
and
\alpha<\kappa
we have
s\restriction\gamma_{\alpha}\in T
, by the pigeonhole principle for every
i<\kappa
there is
s(i)\in\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i}}(T)
such that

|\{t\in\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i+1}}(T)\mid s(i)\subseteq t\}|>\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i}}(T).

Since we assumed
|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i}}(T)|>\kappa
and since for every
i,j<\kappa
there is at most one
t\in\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i+1}}(T)
such that
t\subseteq s(j)
, we have that

Z_{i}=\{t\in\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i+1}}(T)\mid s(i)\subseteq t\text{ and for every }j<\kappa\text{ we have }t\nsubseteq s(j)\}

has still size
|Z_{i}|>|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha_{i}}(T)|
for every
i<\kappa
. Since
\lambda=\sup_{\alpha<\kappa}|\mathrm{Lev}_{\alpha}(T)|
, we have that
|\bigcup_{i<\kappa}Z_{i}|=\lambda
, and thus

\{\mathbf{N}_{t}(X)\mid t\in\bigcup_{i<\kappa}Z_{i}\}

is a family of disjoint basic clopen sets of size
\lambda
, as wanted.
∎

Generalized descriptive set theory at a cardinal
\lambda
usually works with (subspaces of) the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, equipped with the bounded topology.
It is customary in this setting to assume

, which implies the weaker
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
, in order to ensure, for example, that

and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern
