2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
both have weight
\lambda
.

Here, we adopt a slightly more general framework: we work with any space of weight
\lambda
of the form
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, for some set
A
and some ordinal
\gamma
, equipped with the bounded topology it inherits from
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
.
This class of spaces is quite broad. For example, it includes all Lebesgue zero-dimensional metric spaces (equivalently, all ultrametric spaces)
[
9
, Theorem 7.3.15 and Exercise 7.3.F]
and all
\mu
-metrizable spaces for
\mu>\omega

[
22
, Theorem 3.3]
of the required weight, among others (see also
[
3
, Theorem 2.2.1]
).

Thanks to Proposition
2.3
, it is evident why in this context of a topological space
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
of weight
\lambda
, the appropriate notion of measure is that of
\lambda^{+}
-measures: any such space contains an open set that can be partitioned into
\lambda
many open sets, making any
\gamma
-measure for
\gamma<\lambda^{+}
not informative enough in this context.
This is particularly relevant when studying the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
without the assumption
\lambda^{<\kappa}=\lambda
, and it shows that the correct objects to study on
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
are
(\lambda^{\prime})^{+}
-measures, rather than
\lambda^{+}
-measures, for
\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda^{<\kappa}
the weight of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
.

Operationally, our setting can be reduced to a more familiar one, thanks to the following well-known fact (see, e.g.,
[
3
, Proposition 2.2.33]
).

Fact 2.4
.

Let
\gamma
be an ordinal, let
A
be a set, and let
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. Let
\lambda
be the weight of
X
and let
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
. Then,
X
is homeomorphic to a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
.

When
X
is not discrete, the cardinal
\kappa
obtained in this way is unique and satisfies
\kappa\leq\lambda
. Indeed, recall that the
character
of a topological space is the smallest cardinal
\kappa
such that every point of
X
has a local base of size at most
\kappa
, and the
additivity
of a topological space is the unique cardinal
\kappa
such that the topology is
\kappa
-additive but not
\kappa^{+}
-additive
1
1

1

Equivalently, the smallest cardinal
\kappa
such that the topology is not closed under intersections of size
\kappa
.
.
Every non-discrete subspace of a space of the form
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
has both character and additivity equal to
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)
. Since these notions are invariant under homeomorphisms, it follows that if
X
is a non-discrete space that embeds both into
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
and into
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\delta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}B^{{\kern-8.67188pt{\delta}\kern 6.383pt}}_{{\kern-13.46297pt\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.383pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\delta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}B^{{\kern-8.67188pt{\delta}\kern 6.383pt}}_{{\kern-13.46297pt\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.383pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\delta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}B^{{\kern-5.62918pt{\delta}\kern 3.96252pt}}_{{\kern-9.05138pt\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.96252pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\delta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}B^{{\kern-4.49702pt{\delta}\kern 2.83037pt}}_{{\kern-7.91922pt\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.83037pt}}}
, then
\operatorname{cof}(\gamma)=\operatorname{cof}(\delta)
, and this number coincides with the character and additivity of
X
.
Moreover, since every
\kappa
-additive space of weight less than
\kappa
is discrete, we also get
\kappa\leq\lambda
for every non-discrete space
X
of weight
\lambda
and additivity
\kappa
.

On the other hand, if
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
is discrete, then
X
embeds into
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, where
\lambda
is the weight of
X
.

Putting these observations together, we obtain the following corollary of Fact
2.4
.

Corollary 2.5
.

Let
\gamma
be an ordinal, let
A
be a set, and let
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
. Let
\lambda
be the weight of
X
. Then, there is a regular cardinal
\kappa\leq\lambda
such that
X
is homeomorphic to a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
.

In particular, without loss of generality we may restrict attention to subspaces
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
of weight
\lambda
, where
\kappa
is regular and
\kappa\leq\lambda
.
For completeness, and in line with standard practice in generalized descriptive set theory, we also allow spaces of weight at most
\lambda
, although most results of this paper become trivial when considering a
\lambda^{+}
-measure on a space of weight strictly less than
\lambda
.

For the rest of the paper, we use the symbols
\lambda
and
\kappa
to denote infinite cardinals such that
\kappa
is regular and
\kappa\leq\lambda
.

We denote by
(X,\tau)
(or
X
for short) a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
of weight (at most)
\lambda
, always equipped with the bounded topology.

In the rest of this subsection, we compare our setup with those adopted in the literature, with particular emphasis on the generalized Baire and Cantor spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
, and on the role and necessity of cardinal assumptions
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
and
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
, and most importantly,
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
.

Classically, generalized descriptive set theory at a cardinal
\lambda
has focused on the study of the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.71252pt{\lambda}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.00087pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.71252pt{\lambda}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.00087pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.01112pt{\lambda}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.50279pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.19446pt{\lambda}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.68613pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
under the assumption
\lambda^{<\lambda}=\lambda
; this condition is equivalent to
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
and
\lambda
regular.
More recent work has aimed at extending the theory to the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
for arbitrary cardinals
\lambda
(possibly singular) satisfying
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
.
This condition implies the weaker
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
.
Since the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
has weight
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}
, and the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
has weight
2^{<\lambda}
, this setup also ensures that both spaces have weight
\lambda
.

In this framework, the same cardinal
\lambda
plays several distinct roles simultaneously.
First, it determines the “dimension”, i.e., the weight, of the spaces under consideration.
Second, it fixes the appropriate “size” of the associated notions to be studied, such as
\lambda^{+}
-Borel sets,
\lambda
-compactness,
\lambda^{+}
-measures, and related concepts.
Finally, together with its cofinality, it is also used to define the base set and the exponent of the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
themselves.

This identification of roles is precisely what made it necessary to adopt additional cardinal assumptions.
As argued after Proposition
2.3
in the context of measures, the right notions for a topological space of the form
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-8.6619pt{\gamma}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-14.243pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-5.59818pt{\gamma}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-9.58469pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-4.54817pt{\gamma}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-8.53468pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
(and in particular for
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
) are arguably those defined relative to its weight.
However, this cardinal is a priori independent of the cardinals appearing in the definition of
X
, and forcing them to coincide amounts to assuming cardinal equalities such as
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
and
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
.

The setup we adopt instead is strictly more general and allows us to work without cardinal assumptions.
First, when
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
and
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
, we recover the usual setting described above, since in this case the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
itself (and all its subspaces) has weight at most
\lambda
, and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
is homeomorphic to a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
.
However, we do not require that
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
, nor that
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
or
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
. As a consequence, we can study the spaces
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and their subspaces even when both cardinal assumptions
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
and
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda
fail, a situation that is usually not covered in generalized descriptive set theory.

Indeed,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.61346pt{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.68387pt\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.61346pt{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.68387pt\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.08324pt{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.13351pt\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.26657pt{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.31685pt\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
always contains subspaces of weight at most
\lambda
, regardless of whether
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}=\lambda
holds or not, and such spaces need not be discrete.
One example is the subspace
X_{0}
of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
described in
[
2
, Equation 2.3]
(or, similarly, the subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
consisting of sequences that are constant except at finitely many coordinates).
A more interesting example is the body
[T]
of a
\lambda
-Kurepa tree
T
(when such a tree exists), since the definition of a
\lambda
-Kurepa tree guarantees that
[T]
has weight at most
\lambda
, independently of whether
\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}=\lambda
holds.
The results developed in our framework apply to all these spaces.

Moreover, our setup also allows us to analyze the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern itself even when the relevant cardinal assumption
