fails.
This already follows from Fact
2.4
, which shows that this space can always be reduced to a subspace of some
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[20.95267pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.75928pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.11378pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-19.46095pt{\operatorname{cof}(\kappa)}\kern , where
\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}
is the weight of

.
Notice that this new

need not satisfy

, and thus we crucially exploit the fact that the cardinal

In practice, the situation is even simpler and more elegant.
Once
\kappa\neq\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
is allowed, up to homeomorphism the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, and more generally any space of the form
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, can be identified with a space of the form
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-6.4485pt{\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-10.88492pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-5.63184pt{\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-10.06825pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
for which the relevant cardinal assumption
(\lambda^{\prime})^{<\kappa}=\lambda^{\prime}
holds, as shown by the following result.

Proposition 2.6
.

Let
\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda^{<\kappa}
. Then,
(\lambda^{\prime})^{<\kappa}=\lambda^{\prime}
, and the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is homeomorphic to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-6.4485pt{\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-10.88492pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-5.63184pt{\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-10.06825pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
.

Proof.

Let
\lambda^{\prime}=\sup\{\lambda^{\gamma}\mid\gamma<\kappa\text{ cardinal}\}
. If
\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda^{\gamma}
for some
\gamma<\kappa
, then

(\lambda^{\prime})^{<\kappa}=\sup\{(\lambda^{\gamma})^{\delta}\mid\delta<\kappa\text{ cardinal}\}=\sup\{\lambda^{\delta}\mid\delta<\kappa\text{ cardinal}\}=\lambda^{\prime}

as wanted.
Assume instead that
\lambda^{\gamma}<\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\kappa
. Then the family
\{\lambda^{\gamma}\mid\gamma<\kappa\text{ cardinal}\}
is cofinal in
\lambda^{\prime}
, so in particular
\operatorname{cof}(\lambda^{\prime})=\kappa
and
\delta^{\gamma}<\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\kappa
and
\delta<\lambda^{\prime}
.
Then, by
[
16
, Theorem 5.20]
we have
(\lambda^{\prime})^{\gamma}=\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\kappa
, as wanted.

To prove that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is homeomorphic to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-6.4485pt{\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-10.88492pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-5.63184pt{\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-10.06825pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
, assume
\kappa\leq\lambda
.

Claim 2.6.1
.

For every
s\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.48848pt{<\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-28.80609pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.48848pt{<\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-28.80609pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.70824pt{<\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-20.07797pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-6.89157pt{<\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-19.2613pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
there is a partition
\mathcal{P}_{s}\subseteq\mathcal{B}
of
\mathbf{N}_{s}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}})
of size
\lambda^{\prime}
made of basic clopen sets.

Proof.

If
\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda^{\gamma}
for some
\gamma<\kappa
, then

\mathcal{P}_{s}=\{\mathbf{N}_{s\conc t}\mid t\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.39105pt{\gamma}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-12.97215pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.39105pt{\gamma}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-12.97215pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.78151pt{\gamma}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-8.76802pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-3.96484pt{\gamma}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-7.95135pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}\}

is as wanted.
Otherwise, assume that the family
\{\lambda^{\gamma}\mid\gamma<\kappa\text{ cardinal}\}
is cofinal in
\lambda^{\prime}
, and let
\{\gamma_{\alpha}\mid\alpha<\kappa\}
be an enumeration of the cardinals below
\kappa
. For every
\alpha<\kappa
, let
\delta_{\alpha}=\lambda^{\gamma_{\alpha}}
.
Define

\mathcal{P}_{s}=\{\mathbf{N}_{s\conc\langle\alpha\rangle}\mid\kappa\leq\alpha<\lambda\}\cup\{\mathbf{N}_{s\conc\langle\alpha\rangle\conc t}\mid t\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-10.13pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-22.49501pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-10.13pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-22.49501pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.52046pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-16.1415pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\gamma_{\alpha}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-6.7038pt{\gamma_{\alpha}}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-15.32483pt\mathchoice{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.83505pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.27582pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.55101pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}},\alpha<\kappa\}.

Since
\kappa\leq\lambda
, the family
\mathcal{P}_{s}
has size
|\mathcal{P}_{s}|=\sup_{\alpha<\kappa}\delta_{\alpha}=\lambda^{\prime}
, and it is a cover of
\mathbf{N}_{s}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}})
made of basic clopen sets by construction, as wanted.
∎

Then, applying Claim
2.6.1
recursively we can obtain a family
\{\mathcal{P}_{\alpha}\mid\alpha<\kappa\}
of partitions of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
made of basic clopen sets such that

•

\hat{\mathcal{B}}=\bigcup_{\alpha<\kappa}\mathcal{P}_{\alpha}
is a basis for
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda}^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
,

•

\mathcal{P}_{\beta}
refines
\mathcal{P}_{\alpha}
for each
\alpha<\beta<\kappa
, and

•

\{P^{\prime}\in\mathcal{P}_{\alpha+1}\mid P\subseteq P^{\prime}\}
has size
\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\alpha<\kappa
and
P\in\mathcal{P}_{\alpha}
.

Then, it is clear that
(\hat{\mathcal{B}},\supseteq)
is isomorphic as a tree to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-13.33597pt{<\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-30.65358pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-13.33597pt{<\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-30.65358pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.17073pt{<\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-21.54047pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-8.35406pt{<\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-20.7238pt\mathchoice{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.208pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.6343pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.73878pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
, and this isomorphism induces a homeomorphism of topological spaces between
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-6.4485pt{\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-10.88492pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-5.63184pt{\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-10.06825pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
(see also
[
3
, Fact 2.1.5]
or
[
15
, Lemma 2.1]
), as wanted.
∎

Notice that Proposition
2.6
relies on our implicit hypothesis
\kappa\leq\lambda
.
If
\lambda=\omega
and
\kappa>\omega
is weakly compact (see
[
16
, Definition 9.8]
), then
2^{<\kappa}=\kappa>\lambda
; however, the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-7.97206pt{\kappa}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.18306pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-7.97206pt{\kappa}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.18306pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.17769pt{\kappa}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-9.6141pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.30626pt{\kappa}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-8.74268pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
(which is homeomorphic to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.92043pt{\kappa}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.13142pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.57767pt{\kappa}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.01408pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.87767pt{\kappa}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.31409pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
) is not homeomorphic to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-9.52486pt{\kappa}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-15.73586pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-6.4485pt{\kappa}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-10.88492pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-5.63184pt{\kappa}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-10.06825pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
for any
\lambda^{\prime}\geq\kappa
, since the former is
\kappa
-Lindelöf whereas the latter is not (see, e.g.,
[
22
]
).
On the other hand, if
\kappa
is not weakly compact, then
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is homeomorphic to
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-7.61604pt{\kappa}\kern 4.68175pt}}_{{\kern-13.82704pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.68175pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.95085pt{\kappa}\kern 2.82318pt}}_{{\kern-9.38727pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.82318pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\kappa^{{\kern-4.14423pt{\kappa}\kern 2.01656pt}}_{{\kern-8.58064pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.01656pt}}}
for every
\lambda
satisfying
2\leq\lambda\leq\kappa
; thus in this case Proposition
2.6
also holds without the hypothesis
\kappa\leq\lambda
.

We can obtain an analogue of Proposition
2.6
for the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
as well.
First, it is well known that while
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
may not itself be a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, under the assumption
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
it is always homeomorphic to a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
(see, e.g.,
[
3
, Proposition 2.2.54]
).

Fact 2.7
.

Assume
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
. Then
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
is homeomorphic to a closed subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-17.64862pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-45.79637pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-12.10835pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-32.21388pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-11.29169pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-31.39722pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
.

Following Proposition
2.6
, we can obtain a similar result regardless of the assumption
2^{<\lambda}=\lambda
.

Proposition 2.8
.

Let
\lambda^{\prime}=2^{<\lambda}
. Then,
(\lambda^{\prime})^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\lambda^{\prime}
, and the space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}
is homeomorphic to a closed subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-19.49611pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-47.64386pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-19.49611pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 6.59056pt}}_{{\kern-47.64386pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.59056pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-13.57085pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 4.32083pt}}_{{\kern-33.67638pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.32083pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{\prime}}^{{\kern-12.75418pt{\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}\kern 3.50417pt}}_{{\kern-32.85971pt\mathchoice{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[21.01399pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[12.79443pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[9.1389pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.50417pt}}}
.

Proof.

Let
\lambda^{\prime}=\sup\{2^{\gamma}\mid\gamma<\lambda\text{ cardinal}\}
. If
\lambda^{\prime}=2^{\gamma}
for some
\gamma<\lambda
, then

\lambda^{\prime}\leq(\lambda^{\prime})^{<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)}=\sup\{(2^{\gamma})^{\delta}\mid\delta<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)\text{ cardinal}\}\leq\sup\{2^{\delta}\mid\delta<\lambda\text{ cardinal}\}=\lambda^{\prime}

as wanted.
Assume instead that
2^{\gamma}<\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\lambda
. Then the family
\{2^{\gamma}\mid\gamma<\lambda\text{ cardinal}\}
is cofinal in
\lambda^{\prime}
, so in particular
\operatorname{cof}(\lambda^{\prime})=\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
and
\delta^{\gamma}<\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
and
\delta<\lambda^{\prime}
.
Then, by
[
16
, Theorem 5.20]
we have
(\lambda^{\prime})^{\gamma}=\lambda^{\prime}
for every
\gamma<\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
.

The remaining part of the proof proceeds essentially as in the usual argument used to embed the Cantor space into the Baire space.
Let
\kappa=\operatorname{cof}(\lambda)
and let
(\lambda_{i})_{i<\kappa}
be a cofinal sequence of ordinals in
\lambda
. Then, the family
\hat{\mathcal{B}}=\bigcup_{i<\kappa}\{\mathbf{N}_{s}(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern is a basis for

. Since

closed under initial segment such that

is isomorphic (as a tree) to

.
This isomorphism induces a homeomorphism of topological spaces between
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-6.95558pt{\lambda}\kern 3.98613pt}}_{{\kern-13.24393pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.98613pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-4.60278pt{\lambda}\kern 2.45pt}}_{{\kern-9.09445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.45pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\lambda}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}2^{{\kern-3.90279pt{\lambda}\kern 1.75pt}}_{{\kern-8.39445pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.75pt}}}

), as wanted.
∎

This will allow us, in Section
8
, to extend our work to the generalized Cantor space as well.

2.2.
Totally ordered monoids

We now recall some basic notions and definitions about totally ordered monoids. Our main reference here is
[
13
, Chapter X]
.

A
totally ordered monoid
is a structure
\mathbb{S}=(S,0,{+},{\leq})
such that

(1)

+
is a binary associative operation on
\mathbb{S}
(i.e.,
(S,{+})
is a semigroup),

(2)

0
is the neutral element of the operation
+
(i.e.,
(S,{+},0)
is a monoid),

(3)

(S,{\leq})
is a total order,

(4)

the order
{\leq}
is (weakly) translation invariant (i.e., for every
a,b,c,d\in\mathbb{S}
, if
a\leq b
and
c\leq d
, then
a+c\leq b+d
).

A
totally ordered group

\mathbb{G}=(G,0,+,\leq)
is a totally ordered monoid such that
(G,+)
is a group.

The
positive cone

\mathbb{S}^{+}
is defined as
\mathbb{S}^{+}=\{s\in\mathbb{S}\mid 0<s\}
, and the
degree of
\mathbb{S}
, denoted by
\Deg(\mathbb{S})
, is the coinitiality of the positive cone
\mathbb{S}^{+}
with respect to
\leq
, i.e. the minimal size of a set
A\subseteq\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that for every
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
there is some
a\in A
satisfying
a\leq\varepsilon
. By definition,
\Deg(\mathbb{S})
is always a regular cardinal. We say that a totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
is
positively ordered
if
\mathbb{S}^{+}\neq\emptyset
and
\mathbb{S}=\mathbb{S}^{+}\cup\{0\}
, i.e. if
0
is the minimum of the order and
\mathbb{S}
is not the trivial monoid
\{0\}
. Notice that for every totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
, we have that
\mathbb{S}^{+}\cup\{0\}
is a positively totally ordered monoid (or the trivial monoid
\{0\}
). Since for the purpose of this paper we only work with
\mathbb{S}^{+}\cup\{0\}
, we can restrict our attention to positively ordered monoids.

A subset
A\subseteq\mathbb{S}
of a positively totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
is called
Archimedean
if for every
a,b\in A\setminus\{0\}
such that
a<b
there is
n\in\omega
such that
n\cdot a\geq b
, where
n\cdot a=a+...+a
denotes the sum of
n
-many elements equal to
a
.
We call
\mathbb{S}
Archimedean if
A=\mathbb{S}
is Archimedean.
For
a,b\in\mathbb{S}
with
a<b
, we write
a\ll b
when, instead,
n\cdot a<b
for every
n\in\omega
. This way, a (positively totally ordered) monoid is Archimedean if and only if
a\ll b
implies
a=0
.

Definition 2.9
.

A positively totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
is said to be
initially Archimedean
if there is
a\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that the open interval
(0,a)
is Archimedean.

Definition 2.10
.

Let
\mathbb{S}=(S,0,+,\leq)
be a positively totally ordered monoid. Let
\bar{a}=\langle a_{i}\mid i<\gamma\rangle
be a sequence of elements in
\mathbb{S}
. We say that
\bar{a}
is:

(1)

decreasing
if
a_{i}\leq a_{j}
for all
j<i<\gamma
,

(2)

strictly decreasing
if
a_{i}<a_{j}
for all
j<i<\gamma
,

(3)

strongly decreasing
if
a_{i}+a_{i}<a_{j}
for all
j<i<\gamma
,

(4)

nowhere Archimedean (decreasing)
if
a_{i}\ll a_{j}
for all
j<i<\gamma
.

Remark 2.11
.

Note that, for a sequence
\bar{a}
of elements in a positively totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
, we have

\text{\ref{def:nowhere-archimedean}}\Rightarrow\text{\ref{def:strongly-decreasing}}\Rightarrow\text{\ref{def:strictly-decreasing}}\Rightarrow\text{\ref{def:decreasing}}.

The following lemma shows that every monoid is either pathological, initially Archimedean of countable degree, or non-Archimedean in a strong way.

Lemma 2.12
.

Let
\mathbb{S}
be a positively totally ordered monoid.
Then, exactly one of the following holds:

(1)

There is
c\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that
b+b\geq c
for every
b\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
.

(2)

\Deg(\mathbb{S})=\omega
and
\mathbb{S}^{+}
contains a countable strongly decreasing Archimedean sequence
\bar{a}=\langle a_{i}\mid i<\omega\rangle
coinitial in
\mathbb{S}^{+}
.

(3)

\Deg(\mathbb{S})=\delta\geq\omega
and
\mathbb{S}^{+}
contains a nowhere Archimedean decreasing sequence
\bar{a}=\langle a_{i}\mid i<\delta\rangle
coinitial in
\mathbb{S}^{+}
.

Proof.

Assume that we are not in the first case. Then, for every
c\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
there is
b\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that
b+b<c
. This implies that
\delta=\Deg(\mathbb{S})\geq\omega
is infinite (and regular). Let thus
\bar{a}=\langle a_{i}\mid i<\delta\rangle
be coinitial in
\mathbb{S}^{+}
, with
\delta=\Deg(\mathbb{S})
.

We recursively define a strongly decreasing subsequence of
\bar{a}
. Let
\alpha_{0}=0
. Now consider
i<\delta
, and assume that we have already defined
\alpha_{j}\geq j
for all
j<i
. Let
\gamma=\sup\{\alpha_{j}\mid j<i\}
. We have that
i\leq\gamma<\delta
, by the regularity of
\delta
. Since
\gamma<\delta=\Deg(\mathbb{S})
, there is
c\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that
c\leq a_{j}
for every
j\leq\gamma
. Thus, by assumption, we can find some
b\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that
b+b<c
. Now, if it exists, let
b_{\gamma}
be such that
b_{\gamma}\ll c
. Otherwise, let
b_{\gamma}=b
. Since
\bar{a}
is coinitial in
\mathbb{S}^{+}
, there is an
\alpha_{i}<\delta
such that
a_{\alpha_{i}}\leq b_{\gamma}
, and thus
a_{\alpha_{i}}+a_{\alpha_{i}}\leq b_{\gamma}+b_{\gamma}<a_{j}
for every
j\leq\gamma
. This also implies
\alpha_{i}>\gamma
. It follows that the sequence
\bar{a}^{\prime}=\langle a_{\alpha_{i}}\mid i<\delta\rangle
obtained in this way is strongly decreasing and coinitial.

Now suppose first that
for every
c\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
there is
b\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that
b\ll c
.
By construction, this implies that
\bar{a}^{\prime}=\langle a_{\alpha_{i}}\mid i<\delta\rangle
is nowhere Archimedean, as wanted.

Conversely, assume that there is
c\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
such that for all
b\in\mathbb{S}^{+}
we have
n\cdot b\geq c
for some
n\in\omega
.
This implies that the whole interval
[0,c]
is Archimedean.
Notice that if
\bar{a}^{\prime}=\langle a_{\alpha_{i}}\mid i<\delta\rangle
is a strongly decreasing sequence converging to
0
for an uncountable cardinal
\delta
, then
\bar{a}^{\prime}=\langle a_{\alpha_{i}}\mid i<\delta,i\text{ limit}\rangle
is a nowhere Archimedean sequence of the same length converging to
0
. Thus, in order for
[0,c]
to be Archimedean, we must have
\Deg(\mathbb{S})=\delta=\omega
.
Let
j<\delta
be such that
a_{\alpha_{j}}<c
. Then, we have that
\bar{a}^{\prime}=\langle a_{\alpha_{j+i}}\mid i<\omega\rangle
is Archimedean, strongly decreasing, and strongly convergent to
0
, as wanted.
∎

Notice that monoids of infinite, countable degree
\Deg(\mathbb{S})=\omega
can be of any of the three types described in Lemma
2.12
. In this case, the only thing that makes the three cases mutually exclusive is the behavior of the operation
+
.

Following the notation from
[
23
]
, we call
0
-continuous the non-pathological monoids, i.e. those that satisfy point
(2)
or point
(3)
of Lemma
2.12
.
The name is based on the fact that, in a
0
-continuous monoid
(\mathbb{S},0,+,\leq)
, the operation
+
is continuous at
0
: whenever two sequences of the same length converge to
0
in the order topology, their point-wise sum also converges to
0
.

Definition 2.13
.

We call
0
-continuous
a totally ordered monoid
\mathbb{S}
of infinite degree such that
\mathbb{S}^{+}
contains a strongly decreasing coinitial sequence.

From Lemma
2.12
, we also get the following interesting corollary.

Corollary 2.14
.

Every
0
-continuous positively totally ordered monoid of uncountable degree is not Archimedean (and not even initially Archimedean).

Notice that (densely ordered) groups are always
0
-continuous, and if a monoid contains a dense,
0
-continuous submonoid, then it is
0
-continuous itself.

3.
Infinite operations on monoids

To define
\lambda^{+}
-measures, we need a structure where sums of
\lambda
-many elements can be computed: ideally, a monoid with a sum of arity at least
\lambda
.
Infinitary operations and their properties have been widely studied (mostly for the case of groups and fields), even with a specific focus on the purpose of generalizing analysis and descriptive set theory (see, e.g.,
[
27
]
). As anticipated in the introduction, we work here with a definition of infinitary sum that requires only minimal axioms.

Recall that for every set
A
, a sequence of elements of
A
is a function from an ordinal
\gamma
into
A
.
We denote by

the class of sequences of elements of

of any length.
Given a sequence
s=(s_{\alpha})_{\alpha<\gamma}\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-16.07475pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-37.96413pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-16.07475pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-37.96413pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-10.89307pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-26.52832pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-9.84306pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-25.47832pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}
, a sequence
t
is a
subsequence
of
s
if there is a set
I=\{\alpha_{i}\mid i<\delta\}\subseteq\gamma
such that
\alpha_{i}<\alpha_{j}
for all
i<j<\delta
and

.
Given
B\subseteq A
, denote by
s\restriction B
the subsequence of
s
obtained by removing all elements outside of
B
from
s
, i.e.
s\restriction B=(s_{\alpha_{i}})_{i<\delta}
where
\{\alpha_{i}\mid i<\delta\}
is the increasing enumeration of the ordinals of
\{\alpha<\gamma\mid s_{\alpha}\in B\}
.
A
reordering
or
permutation
of
s
is a sequence of the form
(s_{\pi(j)})_{j<\delta}
for some bijection
\pi:\delta\to\gamma
.

the extension of the binary operation of concatenation of sequences

to all sequences of sequences in

On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-16.07475pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 6.01392pt}}_{{\kern-37.96413pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 6.01392pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-10.89307pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-26.52832pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.675pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{<{\sf On}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}A^{{\kern-9.84306pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-25.47832pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.625pt}}}})^{{\kern-206.37791pt{<{\sf On}}\kern 199.15985pt}}_{{\kern-222.01317pt\mathchoice{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.01947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.94972pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.10695pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 199.15985pt}}}
.
Formally, we define
\Conc(\emptyset)=\emptyset
. Then, proceeding recursively on the length, given a sequence
\bar{s}=\langle s^{\alpha}\mid\alpha<\beta\rangle
of sequences
