is continuous as well, since
(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}},\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is. This concludes the proof.
∎

Remarkably, the measure obtained in Proposition
5.3
comes from a classical measure on a Polish space that is extended to the whole space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
via the operation
\uparrow
from Definition
4.17
.
Obviously, the same construction could be carried out starting from an arbitrary
\omega^{+}
-measure taking values in a monoid different from
\mathbb{R}_{\infty}
. This naturally raises the question of whether there are any further possibilities, which breaks down into two separate problems:
is every
\lambda
-Dirac
\lambda^{+}
-measure necessarily
\omega_{1}
-Dirac?
And is every
\omega_{1}
-Dirac weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space induced by some measure on a “small” subspace?

We anticipate that the results of the next sections will give a positive answer to the first question:
\omega_{1}
-Dirac measures are essentially the only continuous
\lambda
-Dirac measure that exist, and they exist only when
\omega<\lambda<\mathfrak{c}
.
In fact, on the one hand if
\lambda\geq\mathfrak{c}
, then not even continuous
\lambda
-Dirac

\lambda^{+}
-measures can exist, by Theorem
1.3
.
On the other hand, if
\lambda<\mathfrak{c}
, then every continuous
\lambda^{+}
-measure is a sum of disjoint
\omega_{1}
-Dirac
\lambda^{+}
-measures on its kernel, by Corollary
6.11
.

In the following, we will give a positive answer to the second question as well, i.e., we are going to show that every
\omega_{1}
-Dirac weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space is “essentially”
induced by some measure on a “small” subspace. To state this, however, we first need
to make every notion precise: we must specify what kind of
small space we are aiming for (separable, second countable, or Polish), what kind of
measure we consider on this small subspace (an
\omega^{+}
-measure or a
\lambda^{+}
-measure), and what we mean by “essentially”.

Let us first introduce a formal definition and compare it to the other possibilities.

Definition 5.4
.

Given a subspace
Y\subseteq X
, a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is called
Y
-induced
if there exists a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
such that
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X
.

We say that
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is
countable-induced
if it is
Y
-induced for some separable subspace
Y\subseteq X
.

By Lemma
4.19
, the structure
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
can always be taken to be precisely
(Y,\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y,\mu\downarrow Y)
.
Indeed, suppose that
Y\subseteq X
and that
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
and
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
are weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure spaces satisfying
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X
.
Then
Y
is essentially null in
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
.
It follows that
\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y\subseteq(\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X)\downarrow Y=\mathfrak{N}
and
\mu\downarrow Y\subseteq(\nu\uparrow X)\downarrow Y=\nu
, which shows that
(Y,\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y,\mu\downarrow Y)
is itself a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space, and moreover that
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq(\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y)\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq(\mu\downarrow Y)\uparrow X
.

Remark 5.5
.

Let
Y\subseteq X
, let
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
be weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space. Then
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is
Y
-induced if and only if
(Y,\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y,\mu\downarrow Y)
is a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space.

In particular, countable-induced weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure spaces
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
that come from a classical measure on
Y
are those where
\mu
takes values in
\mathbb{S}=\mathbb{R}_{\infty}
and
\mu\downarrow Y
can be extended to a
\sigma
-algebra containing
\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y
.

In Definition
5.4
, we used the density (
separable
) as a way to measure smallness. However, we could have used, equivalently, the weight (
second countable
), or even the cellularity,
by Proposition
2.3
.
Moreover, every second countable subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is homeomorphic to a subspace of the Baire space
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
.
When
\kappa>\omega
, this is because
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is
\omega_{1}
-additive, and hence all its second countable subspaces are necessarily countable and discrete; when
\kappa=\omega
, then
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
and all its subspaces are metrizable and zero-dimensional, and the conclusion follows, for example, from
[
17
, Theorem 7.8]
.
In summary, we obtain the following.

Remark 5.6
.

For
Y\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, the following are equivalent:

•

Y
is separable,

•

Y
is second countable,

•

Y
is homeomorphic to a subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
.

In particular, a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is countable-induced if and only if it is
Y
-induced for some second-countable subspace
Y\subseteq X
, if and only it is
f[Y]
-induced for some subspace
Y\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
and embedding
f:Y\to X
.

Regarding the choice of the complexity of
Y
in
X
, we have the following.

Proposition 5.7
.

Let
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
be a continuous weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space.
Then,
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is countable-induced if and only if it is
Y
-induced for some separable closed
Y\subseteq X
.

In particular, if
X
is
G_{\delta}
in
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, then
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is countable-induced if and only if it is
Y
-induced by a
Polish
subspace
Y\subseteq X
.

Proof.

Let
Y\subseteq X
be a separable subspace such that
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
is a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space and
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X
.
Then,
\cl(Y)
is separable as well. Also, by Definition
4.17
of
\uparrow
we get
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X=(\mathfrak{N}\uparrow\cl(Y))\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X=(\nu\uparrow\cl(Y))\uparrow X
.

Thus, to prove the first part of the statement it is enough to check that
(\cl(Y),\mathfrak{N}\uparrow\cl(Y),\nu\uparrow\cl(Y))
is a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space.
By Lemma
4.20
, we get that Axioms
(M1)
-
(M4)
are satisfied.
Now for every
x\in\cl(Y)
, since
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is continuous we have
x\in\mathfrak{M}
and
\mu(x)=0
, thus

\mu(x)=\inf_{i<\gamma}\mu(A_{i})=\inf_{i<\gamma}(\nu\uparrow\cl(Y))(A_{i})

for every (open) local basis
\{A_{i}\mid i<\gamma\}
at
x
of order type
\gamma<\lambda^{+}
, and Axiom
(M5)
holds as wanted.

For the second part of the statement, recall that every countable, discrete space is Polish. Thus, when
\kappa>\omega
, any separable subspace of
X\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is Polish.
If instead
\kappa=\omega
and
X
is
G_{\delta}
in
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
, then every closed subspace of
X
is
G_{\delta}
in
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
as well, thus Polish (see, e.g.,
[
9
, Theorem 4.3.23]
).
∎

We note that for
\gamma
-Dirac measures as well we can relax the hypotheses on the complexity of the subset of small weight.

Proposition 5.8
.

Let
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
be a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space, and let
\gamma
be a cardinal. Then,
\mu
is
\gamma
-Dirac if and only if there is a subspace
Y\subseteq X
(not necessarily closed) of weight
\weight(Y)<\gamma
such that
X\setminus Y
is measurable and
\mu(X\setminus Y)=0
.

Proof.

If
\mu
is
\gamma
-Dirac, then
\operatorname{supp}(\mu)\subseteq X
has weight
\weight(\operatorname{supp}(\mu))<\gamma
by definition, and
X\setminus\operatorname{supp}(\mu)
is measurable since it is open, thus
\mu(X\setminus Y)=0
by Corollary
4.8
.

On the other hand, given a subspace
Y\subseteq X
of weight
\weight(Y)<\gamma
such that
X\setminus Y
is measurable and
\mu(X\setminus Y)=0
, we have that
X\setminus\cl(Y)
is measurable as well, since it is open, and
\mu(X\setminus\cl(Y))\leq\mu(X\setminus Y)=0
by Axiom
(M3)
. Since for any subspace of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.67737pt{\kappa}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-13.88837pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.98601pt{\kappa}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.42242pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\kappa}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.16934pt{\kappa}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-8.60576pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.68175pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.82318pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.01656pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
the weight and the density coincide, by Proposition
2.3
, and any set has the same density of its closure, we get
\weight(\cl(Y))=\density(\cl(Y))=\density(Y)=\weight(Y)<\gamma
. The fact that
X\setminus\cl(Y)
is an open set of measure zero implies that
\operatorname{supp}(\mu)\subseteq\cl(Y)
, thus
\weight(\operatorname{supp}(\mu))\leq\weight(\cl(Y))<\gamma
, as wanted.
∎

Finally, regarding the choice between
\omega^{+}
-measures and
\lambda^{+}
-measures in Definition
5.4
, note that we adopted the more restrictive notion, since every
\lambda^{+}
-measure is automatically an
\omega^{+}
-measure.
Therefore, showing that every
\omega_{1}
-Dirac measure is
Y
-induced by a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space for some separable
Y\subseteq X
is more informative than showing that every
\omega_{1}
-Dirac measure is “
Y
-induced” by a weak
\omega^{+}
-measure space for some separable subspace
Y\subseteq X
.

Nevertheless, we notice that the two choices are equivalent for
\lambda^{+}
-partitionable weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure spaces.

Proposition 5.9
.

Let
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
be a
\lambda^{+}
-partitionable weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space. Then
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is countable-induced if and only if there exists a weak
\omega^{+}
-measure space
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
for some
Y\subseteq X
such that
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X
.

Proof.

One direction is clear, as every weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure is automatically a weak
\omega^{+}
-measure as well.
So assume there exists a weak
\omega^{+}
-measure space
(Y,\mathfrak{N},\nu)
for some
Y\subseteq X
such that
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}\uparrow X
and
\mu\subseteq\nu\uparrow X
.
Let
\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}=\mathfrak{M}\downarrow X
, by assumption we have
\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}
, and
\mathfrak{M}\subseteq\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}\uparrow X
. Therefore, it is enough to prove that
(Y,\mathfrak{N}^{\prime},\nu)
is a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space.

Since
\mathfrak{M}
is closed under unions of size
\lambda
, we get that
\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}=\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y
is as well, and thus
(Y,\mathfrak{N}^{\prime})
is a weakly
\lambda^{+}
-measurable space. Also,
\nu
satisfies Axioms
(M1)
-
(M3)
and Axiom
(M5)
, since it is an
\omega^{+}
-measure.
So we only need to check Axiom
(M4)
. Let
(A_{i})_{i<\gamma}
be a family of disjoint sets of
\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}
of length
\gamma<\lambda^{+}
. Then, there is a family of sets
(A^{\prime}_{i})_{i<\gamma}
in
\mathfrak{M}
such that
A^{\prime}_{i}\cap Y=A_{i}
for every
i<\gamma
. Since
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
is
\lambda^{+}
-partitionable and
\gamma<\lambda^{+}
, there is a family
\mathcal{P}
of disjoint sets of
\mathfrak{M}
of size
|\mathcal{P}|<\lambda^{+}
refining
\{A^{\prime}_{i}\mid i<\gamma\}
. Let
\mathcal{P}_{0}=\{P\in\mathcal{P}\mid P\cap Y\subseteq A_{0}\}
and for
0<i<\gamma
, let
\mathcal{P}_{i}=\{P\in\mathcal{P}\mid P\cap Y\subseteq A_{i},P\cap Y\neq\emptyset\}
. Since both
\mathcal{P}
and
\{A_{i}=A^{\prime}_{i}\cap Y\mid i<\gamma\}
are made of disjoint sets, and
\mathcal{P}
refines
\{A^{\prime}_{i}\mid i<\gamma\}
, we get that
\{\mathcal{P}_{i}\mid i<\gamma\}
is a partition of
\mathcal{P}
and
\bigcup\mathcal{P}_{i}\cap Y=A_{i}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\nu(\bigcup_{i<\gamma}A_{i})=\nu(\bigcup\mathcal{P}\cap Y)

\displaystyle=\mu(\bigcup\mathcal{P})=\sum_{i<\gamma}\mu(\bigcup\mathcal{P}_{i})=\sum_{i<\gamma}\nu(\bigcup\mathcal{P}_{i}\cap Y)=\sum_{i<\gamma}\nu(A_{i})

as wanted.
∎

Without any assumption on
\mathfrak{M}
, we cannot expect an (
\omega_{1}
-Dirac) weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
to be countable-induced, simply because weakly
\lambda^{+}
-measurable spaces allow too much
freedom in adding sets: we can always “dirty” the measurable structure
\mathfrak{M}
by
adding sets to it in such a way that it cannot be restricted to any separable subset.

Proposition 5.10
.

Assume
\omega<\lambda<\mathfrak{c}
.
Then, there is an
\omega_{1}
-Dirac, continuous weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}},\mathfrak{M},\mu)
on
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
that is not countable-induced.

Proof.

By Proposition
5.3
and its proof, there is an
\omega_{1}
-Dirac weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space
(X,\mathfrak{M},\mu)
on
X=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
such that

is the extension to

-measurable structure

on

.

we have

-many times every uncountable closed subsets of

. Pick recursively elements

Then
\mathcal{D}
is closed under arbitrary unions, and thus

\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}=\mathfrak{M}\cup\{D\cup M\mid M\in\mathfrak{M},D\in\mathcal{D}\}

is a weakly
\lambda^{+}
-measurable structure on
X
.

Define
\mu^{\prime}(M)=\mu(\mathrm{int}_{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-6.83258pt{\omega}\kern for every

.
Notice that this way, we have
\mu^{\prime}(M)=\mu(M)
for every
M\in\mathfrak{M}
.
It is easy to check then that
\mu^{\prime}
satisfies Axioms
(M1)
-
(M2)
and Axiom
(M5)
, since
\mu
does. Also, for all
A,B\in\mathfrak{M}
such that
A\subseteq B
, we get
\mathrm{int}(A)\subseteq\mathrm{int}(B)
as well, and thus
\mu^{\prime}(A)=\mu(\mathrm{int}(A))\leq\mu(\mathrm{int}(B))=\mu^{\prime}(B)
and Axiom
(M3)
holds.

To prove Axiom
(M4)
, first we claim that
\mu^{\prime}(M\cup D_{i})=\mu(M)
for every
M\in\mathfrak{M}
and
i\in\{0,1\}
.
By definition of
\mu^{\prime}
, this amounts to check that
\mu(A)=\mu(B)
for
A=\mathrm{int}_{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-6.83258pt{\omega}\kern and

.
Let

be the set of points

such that there is open neighborhood

of

in

satisfying

. Then
F
is countable, since
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
is second countable.
By construction, if
x\in\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}\setminus(F\cup B)
, then every clopen neighborhood
V
of
x
in
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
satisfies
|V\setminus B|\geq\mathfrak{c}
.
Since
V\setminus B
is closed in
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
, we get that
|(V\setminus B)\setminus D_{i}|\geq\mathfrak{c}
.
Since
|(M\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}})\setminus\mathrm{int}_{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-6.83258pt{\omega}\kern 4.43176pt}}_{{\kern-11.62547pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.43176pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-6.83258pt{\omega}\kern 4.43176pt}}_{{\kern-11.62547pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.43176pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-3.95694pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-7.38042pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-3.33447pt{\omega}\kern 1.55612pt}}_{{\kern-6.75795pt\mathchoice{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.43176pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55612pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 1.55612pt}}}}(M\cap\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}})|\leq\lambda<\mathfrak{c}
for every
M\in\mathfrak{M}
, by definition of minimal
\lambda^{+}
-measure space, we get that
|(V\setminus M)\setminus D_{i}|\geq\mathfrak{c}
. Therefore,
V
is not contained in
M\cup D_{i}
. Since this holds for an arbitrary clopen neighborhood of
x
, and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
is zero-dimensional, we get
x\notin A
.
This shows that
A\subseteq F\cup B
, thus
|A\setminus B|\leq\omega
.
Therefore,
\mu(A)=\mu(B)
, as wanted.

Now let
(A_{i})_{i<\gamma}
be a sequence of disjoint measurable sets of length
\gamma<\lambda^{+}
.
Notice that
\lambda>\omega
implies that
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
is not separable, so there are no pairwise disjoint sets from
\mathcal{D}
. Therefore, at most one set of
(A_{i})_{i<\gamma}
belongs to
\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}\setminus\mathfrak{M}
: without loss of generality, assume
A_{0}\in\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}\setminus\mathfrak{M}
.
Since
A_{0}\notin\mathfrak{M}
and by definition of
\mathfrak{M}
and
\mathfrak{M}^{\prime}
, we get that
A_{0}=A^{\prime}_{0}\cup D_{i}
for some
i\in\{0,1\}
and
A_{0}\in\mathfrak{M}
.
Then,
\bigcup_{i<\alpha}A_{i}=A^{\prime}_{0}\cup\bigcup_{0<i<\alpha}A_{i}\cup D_{i}
, and thus

\mu^{\prime}(\bigcup_{i<\alpha}A_{i})=\mu^{\prime}(A^{\prime}_{0}\cup\bigcup_{0<i<\alpha}A_{i}\cup D_{i})=\mu(A^{\prime}_{0}\cup\bigcup_{0<i<\alpha}A_{i})

by the previous argument and since
A^{\prime}_{0}\cup\bigcup_{0<i<\alpha}A_{i}\in\mathfrak{M}
. Since
\mu
is a
\lambda^{+}
-measure and
\{A^{\prime}_{0}\}\cup\{A_{i}\mid 0<i<\gamma\}
is still a family of disjoint open sets in
\mathfrak{M}
, then Axiom
(M4)
follows.

Finally, notice that
\operatorname{supp}(\mu^{\prime})=\operatorname{supp}(\mu)=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 3.05002pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-4.46829pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}_{{\kern-9.26118pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.17859pt}}}
, so
\mu^{\prime}
is still
\omega_{1}
-Dirac. However, for every separable subspace
Y\subseteq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}
(and in particular for
Y=\operatorname{supp}(\mu^{\prime})
), we have that
(Y,\mathfrak{M}\downarrow Y,\mu\downarrow Y)
cannot be a weak
\lambda^{+}
-measure space.
This is because
A_{0}=(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}_{{\kern-14.61424pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 4.74307pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-5.14804pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-4.33138pt{\omega}\kern 2.04167pt}}_{{\kern-9.12427pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 2.04167pt}}}\setminus(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-8.1989pt{\omega}\kern 2.17859pt}}}\cup Y))\cup D_{0}
and
A_{1}=(\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\lambda^{{\kern-7.9042pt{\omega}\kern 2.85834pt}}_{{\kern-9.94093pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}_{{\kern-14.90894pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern 5.03777pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\omega}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\omega^{{\kern-5.33972pt{\omega}\kern 3.05002pt}}_{{\kern-10.13261pt\mathchoice{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.03777pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17859pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\kern
